        function reportBug() {
            var captureElement = document.querySelector("#form1");

            html2canvas(
                captureElement, {
                    scrollY: 0, x: 0, y: 0
                }
            ).then(function (canvas) {
                var bugContent = document.getElementById("divReportBugContent");

                var dataURL = canvas.toDataURL();
                document.getElementById("hiddenScreenshot").value = dataURL;
                document.getElementById("divReportBugModal").style.display = 'block';
            });
        }
        function runDemo() {
            introJs().setOption('showStepNumbers', 'false');
            introJs().start();
        }
    </script>

var theForm = document.forms['form1'];
if (!theForm) {
    theForm = document.form1;
}
function __doPostBack(eventTarget, eventArgument) {
    if (!theForm.onsubmit || (theForm.onsubmit() != false)) {
        theForm.__EVENTTARGET.value = eventTarget;
        theForm.__EVENTARGUMENT.value = eventArgument;
        theForm.submit();
    }
}
//]]>

<script type="text/javascript">
    document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', function () {
        
        var paymentOptions = document.getElementsByName('ctl00$ContentPlaceHolderMain$Method');
        
           if (!document.getElementById('creditCardHintTop')) {
        var creditCardHintTop = document.createElement('div');
        creditCardHintTop.id = 'creditCardHintTop';
        creditCardHintTop.style.width = '100%';
        creditCardHintTop.style.color = 'red';
        creditCardHintTop.style.fontWeight = 'bold';
        creditCardHintTop.style.textAlign = 'center';
        creditCardHintTop.style.padding = '10px';
        creditCardHintTop.style.marginBottom = '10px';
        creditCardHintTop.style.display = 'none'; 
        creditCardHintTop.innerText = '*Credit Card information will be entered on the following screen*';

        
        var creditCardHintBottom = creditCardHintTop.cloneNode(true);
        creditCardHintBottom.id = 'creditCardHintBottom';
        
        
        document.body.insertBefore(creditCardHintTop, document.body.firstChild);
        document.body.appendChild(creditCardHintBottom);
        }

        
        var hintTopElement = document.getElementById('creditCardHintTop');
        var hintBottomElement = document.getElementById('creditCardHintBottom');
        
        
        paymentOptions.forEach(option => {
            option.addEventListener('change', function () {
                var displayStyle = (this.value === 'CreditCard') ? 'block' : 'none';
                hintTopElement.style.display = displayStyle;
                hintBottomElement.style.display = displayStyle;
            });
        });

        
        var selectedOption = document.querySelector("input[name='ctl00$ContentPlaceHolderMain$Method']:checked");
        if (selectedOption && selectedOption.value === 'CreditCard') {
            hintTopElement.style.display = 'block';
            hintBottomElement.style.display = 'block';
        }
    });

<script type="text/javascript">
    document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', function () {
        
        var paymentOptions = document.getElementsByName('ctl00$ContentPlaceHolderMain$Method');
        
           if (!document.getElementById('creditCardHintTop')) {
        var creditCardHintTop = document.createElement('div');
        creditCardHintTop.id = 'creditCardHintTop';
        creditCardHintTop.style.width = '100%';
        creditCardHintTop.style.color = 'red';
        creditCardHintTop.style.fontWeight = 'bold';
        creditCardHintTop.style.textAlign = 'center';
        creditCardHintTop.style.padding = '10px';
        creditCardHintTop.style.marginBottom = '10px';
        creditCardHintTop.style.display = 'none'; 
        creditCardHintTop.innerText = '*Credit Card information will be entered on the following screen*';

        
        var creditCardHintBottom = creditCardHintTop.cloneNode(true);
        creditCardHintBottom.id = 'creditCardHintBottom';
        
        
        document.body.insertBefore(creditCardHintTop, document.body.firstChild);
        document.body.appendChild(creditCardHintBottom);
        }

        
        var hintTopElement = document.getElementById('creditCardHintTop');
        var hintBottomElement = document.getElementById('creditCardHintBottom');
        
        
        paymentOptions.forEach(option => {
            option.addEventListener('change', function () {
                var displayStyle = (this.value === 'CreditCard') ? 'block' : 'none';
                hintTopElement.style.display = displayStyle;
                hintBottomElement.style.display = displayStyle;
            });
        });

        
        var selectedOption = document.querySelector("input[name='ctl00$ContentPlaceHolderMain$Method']:checked");
        if (selectedOption && selectedOption.value === 'CreditCard') {
            hintTopElement.style.display = 'block';
            hintBottomElement.style.display = 'block';

<script type="text/javascript">
    document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', function () {
        
        var paymentOptions = document.getElementsByName('ctl00$ContentPlaceHolderMain$Method');
        
           if (!document.getElementById('creditCardHintTop')) {
        var creditCardHintTop = document.createElement('div');
        creditCardHintTop.id = 'creditCardHintTop';
        creditCardHintTop.style.width = '100%';
        creditCardHintTop.style.color = 'red';
        creditCardHintTop.style.fontWeight = 'bold';
        creditCardHintTop.style.textAlign = 'center';
        creditCardHintTop.style.padding = '10px';
        creditCardHintTop.style.marginBottom = '10px';
        creditCardHintTop.style.display = 'none'; 
        creditCardHintTop.innerText = '*Credit Card information will be entered on the following screen*';

        
        var creditCardHintBottom = creditCardHintTop.cloneNode(true);
        creditCardHintBottom.id = 'creditCardHintBottom';
        
        
        document.body.insertBefore(creditCardHintTop, document.body.firstChild);
        document.body.appendChild(creditCardHintBottom);
        }

        
        var hintTopElement = document.getElementById('creditCardHintTop');
        var hintBottomElement = document.getElementById('creditCardHintBottom');
        
        
        paymentOptions.forEach(option => {
            option.addEventListener('change', function () {
                var displayStyle = (this.value === 'CreditCard') ? 'block' : 'none';
                hintTopElement.style.display = displayStyle;
                hintBottomElement.style.display = displayStyle;
            });
        });

        
        var selectedOption = document.querySelector("input[name='ctl00$ContentPlaceHolderMain$Method']:checked");
        if (selectedOption && selectedOption.value === 'CreditCard') {
            hintTopElement.style.display = 'block';
            hintBottomElement.style.display = 'block';
        }
    });
</script><div><input id="eCheck" type="checkbox" name="ctl00$ContentPlaceHolderMain$eCheck"><label for="eCheck">eCheck (Immediate Draft for CWA)</label></div></div></div></div><div class="flex-container"><div id="AllotmentAdditionalQuestion" class="tableContent" style="display:none;"><div style="font-weight:bold;">Allotment Form Type</div><div class="flex-container" style="width:100%;margin-top:4px;margin-bottom:18px;clear:both;"><div>Choose Allotment Type</div><span id="AllotmentGroupReq" class="requiredField" style="font-size:XX-Large;font-weight:bold;color:red;display:none;">Req</span><ul id="AllotmentGroup" class="radioVertical" dynamiccontrol="True" style="list-style-type:none;">

    document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', function () {
        
        var paymentOptions = document.getElementsByName('ctl00$ContentPlaceHolderMain$Method');
        
           if (!document.getElementById('creditCardHintTop')) {
        var creditCardHintTop = document.createElement('div');
        creditCardHintTop.id = 'creditCardHintTop';
        creditCardHintTop.style.width = '100%';
        creditCardHintTop.style.color = 'red';
        creditCardHintTop.style.fontWeight = 'bold';
        creditCardHintTop.style.textAlign = 'center';
        creditCardHintTop.style.padding = '10px';
        creditCardHintTop.style.marginBottom = '10px';
        creditCardHintTop.style.display = 'none'; 
        creditCardHintTop.innerText = '*Credit Card information will be entered on the following screen*';

        
        var creditCardHintBottom = creditCardHintTop.cloneNode(true);
        creditCardHintBottom.id = 'creditCardHintBottom';
        
        
        document.body.insertBefore(creditCardHintTop, document.body.firstChild);
        document.body.appendChild(creditCardHintBottom);
        }

        
        var hintTopElement = document.getElementById('creditCardHintTop');
        var hintBottomElement = document.getElementById('creditCardHintBottom');
        
        
        paymentOptions.forEach(option => {
            option.addEventListener('change', function () {
                var displayStyle = (this.value === 'CreditCard') ? 'block' : 'none';
                hintTopElement.style.display = displayStyle;
                hintBottomElement.style.display = displayStyle;
            });
        });

        
        var selectedOption = document.querySelector("input[name='ctl00$ContentPlaceHolderMain$Method']:checked");
        if (selectedOption && selectedOption.value === 'CreditCard') {
            hintTopElement.style.display = 'block';
            hintBottomElement.style.display = 'block';
        }
    });

<script type="text/javascript">
    document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', function () {
        
        var paymentOptions = document.getElementsByName('ctl00$ContentPlaceHolderMain$Method');
        
           if (!document.getElementById('creditCardHintTop')) {
        var creditCardHintTop = document.createElement('div');
        creditCardHintTop.id = 'creditCardHintTop';
        creditCardHintTop.style.width = '100%';
        creditCardHintTop.style.color = 'red';
        creditCardHintTop.style.fontWeight = 'bold';
        creditCardHintTop.style.textAlign = 'center';
        creditCardHintTop.style.padding = '10px';
        creditCardHintTop.style.marginBottom = '10px';
        creditCardHintTop.style.display = 'none'; 
        creditCardHintTop.innerText = '*Credit Card information will be entered on the following screen*';

        
        var creditCardHintBottom = creditCardHintTop.cloneNode(true);
        creditCardHintBottom.id = 'creditCardHintBottom';
        
        
        document.body.insertBefore(creditCardHintTop, document.body.firstChild);
        document.body.appendChild(creditCardHintBottom);
        }

        
        var hintTopElement = document.getElementById('creditCardHintTop');
        var hintBottomElement = document.getElementById('creditCardHintBottom');
        
        
        paymentOptions.forEach(option => {
            option.addEventListener('change', function () {
                var displayStyle = (this.value === 'CreditCard') ? 'block' : 'none';
                hintTopElement.style.display = displayStyle;
                hintBottomElement.style.display = displayStyle;
            });
        });

        
        var selectedOption = document.querySelector("input[name='ctl00$ContentPlaceHolderMain$Method']:checked");
        if (selectedOption && selectedOption.value === 'CreditCard') {
            hintTopElement.style.display = 'block';
            hintBottomElement.style.display = 'block';
        }
    });

<script type="text/javascript">
    document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', function () {
        
        var paymentOptions = document.getElementsByName('ctl00$ContentPlaceHolderMain$Method');
        
           if (!document.getElementById('creditCardHintTop')) {
        var creditCardHintTop = document.createElement('div');
        creditCardHintTop.id = 'creditCardHintTop';
        creditCardHintTop.style.width = '100%';
        creditCardHintTop.style.color = 'red';
        creditCardHintTop.style.fontWeight = 'bold';
        creditCardHintTop.style.textAlign = 'center';
        creditCardHintTop.style.padding = '10px';
        creditCardHintTop.style.marginBottom = '10px';
        creditCardHintTop.style.display = 'none'; 
        creditCardHintTop.innerText = '*Credit Card information will be entered on the following screen*';

        
        var creditCardHintBottom = creditCardHintTop.cloneNode(true);
        creditCardHintBottom.id = 'creditCardHintBottom';
        
        
        document.body.insertBefore(creditCardHintTop, document.body.firstChild);
        document.body.appendChild(creditCardHintBottom);
        }

        
        var hintTopElement = document.getElementById('creditCardHintTop');
        var hintBottomElement = document.getElementById('creditCardHintBottom');
        
        
        paymentOptions.forEach(option => {
            option.addEventListener('change', function () {
                var displayStyle = (this.value === 'CreditCard') ? 'block' : 'none';
                hintTopElement.style.display = displayStyle;
                hintBottomElement.style.display = displayStyle;
            });
        });

        
        var selectedOption = document.querySelector("input[name='ctl00$ContentPlaceHolderMain$Method']:checked");
        if (selectedOption && selectedOption.value === 'CreditCard') {
            hintTopElement.style.display = 'block';
            hintBottomElement.style.display = 'block';
        }
    });

<script type="text/javascript">
    document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', function () {
        
        var paymentOptions = document.getElementsByName('ctl00$ContentPlaceHolderMain$Method');
        
           if (!document.getElementById('creditCardHintTop')) {
        var creditCardHintTop = document.createElement('div');
        creditCardHintTop.id = 'creditCardHintTop';
        creditCardHintTop.style.width = '100%';
        creditCardHintTop.style.color = 'red';
        creditCardHintTop.style.fontWeight = 'bold';
        creditCardHintTop.style.textAlign = 'center';
        creditCardHintTop.style.padding = '10px';
        creditCardHintTop.style.marginBottom = '10px';
        creditCardHintTop.style.display = 'none'; 
        creditCardHintTop.innerText = '*Credit Card information will be entered on the following screen*';

        
        var creditCardHintBottom = creditCardHintTop.cloneNode(true);
        creditCardHintBottom.id = 'creditCardHintBottom';
        
        
        document.body.insertBefore(creditCardHintTop, document.body.firstChild);
        document.body.appendChild(creditCardHintBottom);
        }

        
        var hintTopElement = document.getElementById('creditCardHintTop');
        var hintBottomElement = document.getElementById('creditCardHintBottom');
        
        
        paymentOptions.forEach(option => {
            option.addEventListener('change', function () {
                var displayStyle = (this.value === 'CreditCard') ? 'block' : 'none';
                hintTopElement.style.display = displayStyle;
                hintBottomElement.style.display = displayStyle;
            });
        });

        
        var selectedOption = document.querySelector("input[name='ctl00$ContentPlaceHolderMain$Method']:checked");
        if (selectedOption && selectedOption.value === 'CreditCard') {
            hintTopElement.style.display = 'block';
            hintBottomElement.style.display = 'block';
        }
    });

    document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', function () {
        
        var paymentOptions = document.getElementsByName('ctl00$ContentPlaceHolderMain$Method');
        
           if (!document.getElementById('creditCardHintTop')) {
        var creditCardHintTop = document.createElement('div');
        creditCardHintTop.id = 'creditCardHintTop';
        creditCardHintTop.style.width = '100%';
        creditCardHintTop.style.color = 'red';
        creditCardHintTop.style.fontWeight = 'bold';
        creditCardHintTop.style.textAlign = 'center';
        creditCardHintTop.style.padding = '10px';
        creditCardHintTop.style.marginBottom = '10px';
        creditCardHintTop.style.display = 'none'; 
        creditCardHintTop.innerText = '*Credit Card information will be entered on the following screen*';

        
        var creditCardHintBottom = creditCardHintTop.cloneNode(true);
        creditCardHintBottom.id = 'creditCardHintBottom';
        
        
        document.body.insertBefore(creditCardHintTop, document.body.firstChild);
        document.body.appendChild(creditCardHintBottom);
        }

        
        var hintTopElement = document.getElementById('creditCardHintTop');
        var hintBottomElement = document.getElementById('creditCardHintBottom');
        
        
        paymentOptions.forEach(option => {
            option.addEventListener('change', function () {
                var displayStyle = (this.value === 'CreditCard') ? 'block' : 'none';
                hintTopElement.style.display = displayStyle;
                hintBottomElement.style.display = displayStyle;
            });
        });

        
        var selectedOption = document.querySelector("input[name='ctl00$ContentPlaceHolderMain$Method']:checked");
        if (selectedOption && selectedOption.value === 'CreditCard') {
            hintTopElement.style.display = 'block';
            hintBottomElement.style.display = 'block';
        }
    });

    <script>
           //ProgReq#9297 Adding USPS Address Validation API - Charmi Sanghavi 5/02/2024
           var streetAddressParts;
           var zipCodeParts;
           var AppNum = document.getElementById('appNum').value;
           var Address1 = document.getElementById('StreetAddress');
           var ZipCode = document.getElementById('ZipCode');
           var StateField = document.getElementById('State');
           var CityField = document.getElementById('City');
           var addressVerificationSpan = document.createElement("span");
           addressVerificationSpan.id = "addressVerificationSpan";
           var formattedAddressSpan = document.createElement("span");
           formattedAddressSpan.id = "formattedAddressSpan";
           var zipCodeSpan = document.createElement("span");
           zipCodeSpan.id = "zipCodeSpan";
           var agency = 'SM001';
           var policyOption = '';
           var mode = 'M';

           if (typeof (ZipCode) != 'undefined' && ZipCode != null && typeof (StateField) != 'undefined' && StateField != null && typeof (CityField) != 'undefined' && CityField != null) {
               // exists.
               ZipCode.addEventListener("blur", AddressValidator);
               ZipCode.addEventListener("input", AddressValidator);
               Address1.addEventListener("blur", AddressValidator);
               StateField.addEventListener("blur", AddressValidator);
               CityField.addEventListener("blur", AddressValidator);
               Address1.parentNode.appendChild(addressVerificationSpan);
               Address1.parentNode.appendChild(formattedAddressSpan);
               ZipCode.parentNode.appendChild(zipCodeSpan);
           }

           var previousInvalidAddress = "";
           var lastAddressCalled = "";
           var showAlert = false;
           var eventListenerAdded = false;
           var alertShown = false;
           var showAlertMsg = false;
           var eventListenerAddedPre = false;
           function AddressValidator() {
               if (ZipCode.value.trim() === "") {
                   zipCodeSpan.innerHTML = "";
                   lastAddressCalled = "";
                   return;
               }

               var patt = /^\d{5}$/;
               var res = patt.test(ZipCode.value);
               if (res == false) return;

               var currentAddressCall = ZipCode.value + Address1.value + CityField.value + StateField.value;
               if (currentAddressCall == lastAddressCalled) {
                   return;
               }

               lastAddressCalled = currentAddressCall;

               var xhttp = new XMLHttpRequest();
               xhttp.onreadystatechange = function () {
                   if (this.readyState == 4 && this.status == 200) {
                       if (xhttp.responseText.indexOf("|") >= 0) {
                           var responses = xhttp.responseText.split("|");

                           // Update City and State fields with the response values
                           if (responses[1].trim() != "") {
                               CityField.value = responses[1];
                           }
                           if (responses[2].trim() != "") {
                               StateField.value = responses[2];
                           }

                           ValidatorValidate(document.getElementById('CityReq'));
                           ValidatorValidate(document.getElementById('StateReq'));
                       }

                       if (CityField.value != "" && StateField.value != "") {
                           zipCodeSpan.innerHTML = ""; 
                       } else {
                           zipCodeSpan.innerHTML = "<span style='color:red;'>Please enter a valid ZipCode.</span>";
                       }

                       if (typeof callback === "function") {
                           callback();
                       }
                   }
               };

               var requestUrl = "../api/uspsvalidator.ashx?zipcode=" + ZipCode.value + "&address1=" + Address1.value;
               if (CityField.value != "" && StateField.value != "") {
                   requestUrl += "&city=" + CityField.value + "&state=" + StateField.value;
               }

               xhttp.open("GET", requestUrl, true);
               xhttp.send();
           }
           
           if (typeof (ZipCode) != 'undefined' && ZipCode != null && typeof (StateField) != 'undefined' && StateField != null && typeof (CityField) != 'undefined' && CityField != null) {
               // exists.
               ZipCode.addEventListener("blur", GoogleValidator);
               ZipCode.addEventListener("input", GoogleValidator);
               Address1.addEventListener("blur", GoogleValidator);
               StateField.addEventListener("blur", GoogleValidator);
               CityField.addEventListener("blur", GoogleValidator);
               Address1.parentNode.appendChild(addressVerificationSpan);
               Address1.parentNode.appendChild(formattedAddressSpan);
               ZipCode.parentNode.appendChild(zipCodeSpan);
           }
           var lastAddressCalledGoogle = "";
           var lastAddressResponse = null;
           function GoogleValidator() {
               //debugger;
               var patt = /^\d{5}$/;
               var res = patt.test(ZipCode.value);
               if (res == false) return;
               //ProgReq#9297 Adding USPS Address Validation API - Charmi Sanghavi 5/02/2024
               var currentAddressCall = ZipCode.value + Address1.value + CityField.value + StateField.value;
               var addressLine = Address1.value + "," + CityField.value + "," + StateField.value + "," + ZipCode.value;
               console.log(addressLine);
               console.log(currentAddressCall);
               //ProgReq#9297 Adding USPS Address Validation API - Charmi Sanghavi 09/03/2024
               var excludeStates = ["GU", "VI", "MP", "MH", "AS", "JM", "PR"];
               var stateCode = StateField.value.trim().toUpperCase();
               if (excludeStates.includes(stateCode)) {
                   return;
               }
               if (currentAddressCall == lastAddressCalledGoogle && lastAddressResponse) {
                   processAddressResponse(lastAddressResponse);
                   return;
               }
               lastAddressCalledGoogle = currentAddressCall;
               
               var address = {
                   address: {
                       regionCode: "US",
                       addressLines: [addressLine]
                   }
               };

               //var originalAddress = addressLine;
               // var encodedAddress = encodeURIComponent(originalAddress);
              //var googleUrl = `../api/googlevalidator.ashx?&address1=${encodeURIComponent(Address1.value)}&city=${encodeURIComponent(CityField.value)}&state=${encodeURIComponent(StateField.value)}&zipcode=${encodeURIComponent(ZipCode.value)}&appNumber=${encodeURIComponent(AppNum)} `;
              
               var googleUrl = "../api/googlevalidator.ashx?Type=CallApi&address1=" + Address1.value + "&city=" + CityField.value + "&state=" + StateField.value + "&zipcode=" + ZipCode.value + "&appNumber=" + AppNum;

               var xhttp = new XMLHttpRequest();
               xhttp.onreadystatechange = function () {
                   if (this.readyState == 4 && this.status == 200) {
                       var response = JSON.parse(xhttp.responseText);
                       console.log(response);
                       lastAddressResponse = response;
                       processAddressResponse(response);
                   }
               };             
               var appNumber = AppNum;
               var originalAddress = addressLine;           
               console.log(googleUrl);
               xhttp.open("GET", googleUrl, true);
               xhttp.send();           
               //xhttp.open("GET", "http://localhost:7185/api/AddressApi/ValidateApiAddresses/" + appNumber + "/" + originalAddress, true);
               //xhttp.open("GET", "https://devapi.aatx.com:15443/Addressvalidapi/api/AddressApi/ValidateApiAddresses/" + appNumber + "/" + originalAddress, true);
               //xhttp.open("GET", "https://web2.aatx.com/api/AddressValidAPI/api/AddressApi/ValidateApiAddresses/" + appNumber + "/" + originalAddress, true);                          
           }

           function processAddressResponse(response) {
               //debugger;
               console.log(response);
               var currentAddressCall = ZipCode.value + Address1.value + CityField.value + StateField.value;
               var formattedAddress = response.formattedAddress;
               var hasUnconfirmedComponents = response.hasUnconfirmedComponents;
               var validationStatus = response.validationGranularity;
               console.log(validationStatus);
               var dpvFootnote = response.dpvFootnote;
               console.log(dpvFootnote);
               document.getElementById('HiddenValidationStatus').value = validationStatus;
               document.getElementById('HiddenDpvFootNote').value = dpvFootnote;
               if (addressVerificationSpan) {
                   addressVerificationSpan.innerHTML = "";
                   addressVerificationSpan.style.display = "none";

               }
               var hasUnconfirmedComponentHiddenField = document.getElementById('HasUnconfirmedComponentsHiddenField');
               if (hasUnconfirmedComponentHiddenField) {
                   hasUnconfirmedComponentHiddenField.value = hasUnconfirmedComponents ? "True" : "False";
               }

               //ProgReq#9297 Address Validation Charmi Sanghavi 08/22/2024isAddressValid
               //debugger;

               function showAlertMessage(event) {
                   console.log("showAlertMessage called");
                   if (!isAddressValid && lastAddressCalledGoogle === currentAddressCall && !showAlert) {
                       var recommendationMessage = "The address entered could not be verified with USPS. Please enter a valid address to continue the application.";
                       alert(recommendationMessage);
                       event.preventDefault();
                       showAlert = true;

                       Method_2.scrollIntoView({ behavior: "smooth", block: "start" });

                       setTimeout(function () {
                           showAlert = false;
                       },0);
                   }
               }

               function addEventListeners() {
                   console.log("addEventListeners called");
                   if (eventListenerAdded) {
                       console.log("Removing existing event listeners");
                       document.getElementById('BtnContinue').removeEventListener('click', showAlertMessage);
                       document.getElementById('BtnSaveReturn').removeEventListener('click', showAlertMessage);
                   }
                   console.log("Adding event listeners");
                   document.getElementById('BtnContinue').addEventListener('click', showAlertMessage);
                   document.getElementById('BtnSaveReturn').addEventListener('click', showAlertMessage);
                   eventListenerAdded = true;
               }

               if (currentAddressCall !== lastAddressCalledGoogle) {
                   showAlert = true;
               }

               if (zipCodeSpan.innerHTML.includes("Please enter a valid ZipCode.")) {
                   isAddressValid = false;
                   addressVerificationSpan.innerHTML = "<i class='fa fa-close w3-text-red'></i>";
                   addressVerificationSpan.style.display = "block";

                   var addressData = {
                       streetAddress: Address1.value,
                       zipCode: ZipCode.value,
                       city: CityField.value,
                       state: StateField.value
                   };
                   console.log(addressData);

           document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', function () {
               console.log("Document loaded, checking address validation status...");

               function disableFields() {
                   var Address1 = document.getElementById('StreetAddress');
                   var ZipCode = document.getElementById('ZipCode');
                   var StateField = document.getElementById('State');
                   var CityField = document.getElementById('City');

                   if (Address1) {
                       Address1.disabled = true;
                       console.log('Address1 disabled');
                   }
                   if (ZipCode) {
                       ZipCode.disabled = true;
                       console.log('ZipCode disabled');
                   }
                   if (StateField) {
                       StateField.disabled = true;
                       console.log('StateField disabled');
                   }
                   if (CityField) {
                       CityField.disabled = true;
                       console.log('CityField disabled');
                   }
               }

               function checkAddressValidationStatus() {
                   console.log("Checking address validation status");
                   $.ajax({
                       type: "POST",
                       url: "../api/googlevalidator.ashx?Type=CheckAddressValidationVerification&appNumber=" +3114054,
                       contentType: "application/json; charset=utf-8",
                       dataType: "json",
                       success: function (response) {
                           console.log("Response received:", response);
                           if (response.d === true) {
                               console.log("Validation verified, disabling fields...");
                               disableFields();
                           } else {
                               console.log("Validation not verified.");
                           }
                       },
                       error: function (response) {
                           console.error("Error checking address validation status:", response);
                       }
                   });
               }

               checkAddressValidationStatus();
           });

        //ProgReq#9399 PAR Changes Charmi Sanghavi 02/25/2025
        document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', function () {
            var selectedOptionValue = localStorage.getItem('selectedOptionValue');
            var paymentModeDropdown = localStorage.getItem('paymentModeDropdown');
            var paymentTypeButton = document.getElementById('Method');
            var radioButtons = Array.from(document.querySelectorAll('#Method input[type="radio"]')).filter(radioButton => radioButton.id !== 'Method_0');
            console.log('selected option value:', selectedOptionValue);
            console.log('options value:', paymentModeDropdown);
            function updatepaymentTypeButton() {
                var checkedOption = Array.from(radioButtons).find(radioButton => radioButton.checked);
                var selectedPaymentTypeValue = checkedOption ? checkedOption.value : null;
                console.log(selectedPaymentTypeValue);
                if (selectedOptionValue && selectedOptionValue.includes("APP") && paymentModeDropdown && paymentModeDropdown.includes("A")) {
                    radioButtons.forEach(function (radioButton) {
                        if (radioButton.value !== 'Direct') {
                            radioButton.disabled = true;
                            radioButton.parentElement.style.display = "none";
                        } else {
                            radioButton.disabled = false;
                            radioButton.parentElement.style.display = "block";
                            radioButton.checked = true;
                        }
                    });
                } else {
                    radioButtons.forEach(function (radioButton) {
                        radioButton.disabled = false;
                        radioButton.parentElement.style.display = "block";
                    });
                }
            }

        //ProgReq#9949	WebAppMobile - Edit Checks on Certain Input Fields - Charmi Sanghavi 05/28/2025
        document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', function () {
            function disableHeightWeightFields() {
                //ProgReq#10284 REMOVE RESTRICTIONS FOR NORDQUIST/FSP - Charmi Sanghavi 12/16/2025
                var product = document.getElementById('product').value;
                if (product == "FN") {
                    return;
                }
                var height = document.getElementById('Height');
                var weight = document.getElementById('Weight');
                if (height && height.value.trim() !== "") {
                    height.disabled = true;
                    console.log('Height disabled');
                }
                if (weight && weight.value.trim() !== "" && weight.value.length === 3) {
                    weight.disabled = true;
                    console.log('Weight disabled');
                }
            }

            function saveHeightWeight() {
                var heightField = document.getElementById('Height');
                var weightField = document.getElementById('Weight');

                var data = {
                    Height: heightField.value,
                    Weight: weightField.value,
                };

                $.ajax({
                    type: "POST",
                    url: "../api/HeightWeightValidation.ashx?Type=SaveHeightWeightVerification&appNumber=" + 3114054,
                    data: JSON.stringify(data),
                    contentType: "application/json; charset=utf-8",
                    dataType: "json",
                    success: function () {
                        console.log("Height and weight saved successfully.");
                    },
                    error: function () {
                        console.error("Error saving height and weight.");
                    }
                });
            }

            function addHeightWeightVerificationFlag() {
                var validationFlagData = {
                    fieldName: "HeightWeightVerification",
                    fieldValue: "true"
                };

                $.ajax({
                    type: "POST",
                    url: "../api/HeightWeightValidation.ashx?Type=AddHeightWeightVerification&appNumber=" + 3114054,
                    data: JSON.stringify(validationFlagData),
                    contentType: "application/json; charset=utf-8",
                    dataType: "json",
                    success: function () {
                     console.log("Verification flag saved");
                     saveHeightWeight();
                     disableHeightWeightFields();
                 },
                     error: function () {
                     console.error("Error saving verification flag");
                     }
              });
         } 

    function checkHeightWeightVerificationStatus() {
        console.log("Checking Height weight verification status");
        $.ajax({
            type: "POST",
            url: "../api/HeightWeightValidation.ashx?Type=CheckHeightWeightVerification&appNumber=" + 3114054,
            contentType: "application/json; charset=utf-8",
            dataType: "json",
            success: function (response) {
                console.log("Response received:", response);
                if (response.d === false) {
                    var heightField = document.getElementById('Height');
                    var weightField = document.getElementById('Weight');
                    var heightValue = heightField ? heightField.value.trim() : "";
                    var weightValue = weightField ? weightField.value.trim() : "";

                    if (heightValue && weightValue && weightValue.length === 3) {
                        console.log("No verification found. Saving and disabling fields...");
                        addHeightWeightVerificationFlag();
                    } else {
                        console.log("Height or Weight is empty, waiting for user input...");
                    }
                } else {
                    console.log("Verification already exists. Disabling fields.");
                    disableHeightWeightFields();
                }
            },
            error: function (response) {
                console.error("Error checking verification status:", response);
            }
        });
            }

            function tryValidateHeightWeight() {
                var height = document.getElementById('Height')?.value.trim();
                var weight = document.getElementById('Weight')?.value.trim();

                if (height && weight && weight.length === 3) {
                    console.log("Both height and weight are entered. Proceeding with validation...");
                    addHeightWeightVerificationFlag();
                } else {
                    console.log("Waiting for both height and weight to be entered...");
                }
            }

            checkHeightWeightVerificationStatus();

            document.getElementById('Height').addEventListener('input', tryValidateHeightWeight);
            document.getElementById('Weight').addEventListener('input', tryValidateHeightWeight);
        });

        var fndobField = document.getElementById('FNDob');
        if (typeof (fndobField) != 'undefined' && fndobField != null) {
            // exists.
            fndobField.addEventListener("input", FormatDate);
        }
        var FNFirstDeduct = document.getElementById('FNFirstDeduct');
        if (typeof (FNFirstDeduct) != 'undefined' && FNFirstDeduct != null) {
            // exists.

        if (typeof (FNIssueDate) != 'undefined' && FNIssueDate != null) {
            // exists.

        if (typeof (FNExpirationDate) != 'undefined' && FNExpirationDate != null) {
            // exists.

        function FormatDate(e) {

            var patt = /^\d{8}$/;
            var res = patt.test(e.target.value);
            if (res == false) return;
            e.target.value = e.target.value.substring(0, 2) + "/" + e.target.value.substring(2, 4) + "/" + e.target.value.substring(4, 8);
        }

        // ProgReq#9885 - Add bi-monthly bank draft as a mode for Financial Fitness, agency EF004 only - Charmi Sanghavi 08/21/2025
        function populateRequestedDraftDayForEF004() {
            if (agency === "EF004" && mode === "N" && (policyOption === "Regular" || policyOption === "Government")) {

                document.querySelector("ul#SSP").closest(".flex-container").style.display = "none";

                const spans = document.querySelectorAll("span");
                spans.forEach(span => {
                    if (span.textContent.includes("*Select REQUESTED DRAFT DAY only after answering the above question")) {
                        span.style.display = "none";
                    }
                });


                if (mode === "N") {

                    const select = document.getElementById("RequestedDraftDay");
                    if (!select) return;
                    select.innerHTML = "";

                    const values = [1, 15];
                    values.forEach(val => {
                        const option = document.createElement("option");
                        option.value = val;
                        option.textContent = val;
                        select.appendChild(option);
                    });
                }
            }
        }

        document.addEventListener("DOMContentLoaded", function () {
            populateRequestedDraftDayForEF004();
        });
        function checkSSP(thisObject, docId) {
            
            var select = document.getElementById(docId);
            if ($("#RDraftDay").length > 0) {
                var select = document.getElementById('RDraftDay');
            }
            var lastSelectedIndex = select.options[select.selectedIndex].value;
            //console.log(lastSelectedIndex);
            select.options.length = 0;
            var option = document.createElement("option");
            var product = document.getElementById('product').value;

            if (thisObject.value == 'Yes') {
                //if (product != "GG")
                //{
                    option = document.createElement("option");
                    option.text = "1S";
                    select.add(option);
                    option = document.createElement("option");
                    option.text = "3S";
                    select.add(option);
                    option = document.createElement("option");
                //}
                option.text = "2W";
                select.add(option);
                option = document.createElement("option");
                option.text = "3W";
                select.add(option);
                option = document.createElement("option");
                option.text = "4W";
                select.add(option);

            } else {
                option = document.createElement("option");
                option.text = "";
                option.disabled = true;
                option.selected = false;
                select.add(option);
                for (i = 1; i <= 28; i++) {
                    var option = document.createElement("option");
                    option.text = i;
                    select.add(option);
                }
                option = document.createElement("option");
                option.text = "NA";
                option.disabled = false;
                option.selected = true;
                select.add(option);

            }
            select.text = lastSelectedIndex;

        }

        function CheckboxExclusive_ClientValidate(thisObject, anotherCheckbox) {
            if (thisObject.checked && anotherCheckbox.checked) {
                alert('Only one of these options can be selected per application.');
                thisObject.checked = false;
            }
        }

        function DisplayCitizenshipQairOrNot() {
            var SSN = document.getElementById('SSN');
            var CitizenshipQair = document.getElementById('CitizenshipQair');
            if (typeof (CitizenshipQair) != 'undefined' && CitizenshipQair != null) {
                if (typeof (SSN) != 'undefined' && SSN != null && SSN.value.substring(0, 1) === '9')
                    DisplayCitizenshipQair(CitizenshipQair, true);
                else {
                    DisplayCitizenshipQair(CitizenshipQair, false);
                }

            }
        }
        function DisplayCitizenshipQair(citizenshipQair, isDisplayed) {
            if (isDisplayed == true)
                citizenshipQair.style.display = 'block';
            else
                citizenshipQair.style.display = 'none';
            var idStr = "FRQBirthCountryReq,FRQITINReq,FRQCitizenCountryReq,FRQ1LiveUSAReq,FRQ2MoveBackReq,FRQEmployedReq,FRQ3EmployerNameReq,FRQ3HireDateReq,FRQPropertyReq,FRQ4StreetAddressReq,FRQ4CityReq,FRQ4StateReq,FRQ4ZipCodeReq,FRQ5DoctorReq,FRQ5DoctorAddressReq,FRQ5DoctorPhonePhone,FRQ5DoctorPhoneReq,FRQ5DoctorLastSeenReq";
            var objs = null;
            if(idStr !== "")
                objs = idStr.split(",");
            if (objs != null) {
                for (let i = 0; i < objs.length; i++)
                    document.getElementById(objs[i]).enabled = isDisplayed;
            }          
        }

<script type="text/javascript">
//<![CDATA[

       function setError(message) {
    var errorContainer = document.getElementById('errorMessageContainer');

    if (!errorContainer) {
        errorContainer = document.createElement('div');
        errorContainer.id = 'errorMessageContainer';

        
        errorContainer.style.position = 'relative';
        errorContainer.style.width = '100%';
        errorContainer.style.textAlign = 'center';
        errorContainer.style.color = '#d8000c';
        errorContainer.style.padding = '10px';
        errorContainer.style.marginTop = '20px'; // Adds spacing from content
       
        document.body.appendChild(errorContainer);
    }

    errorContainer.textContent = message;
}
    function validateMentalHealthSelection() {
        var container = document.getElementById('QuestMentalInfo');

        
        if (!container || window.getComputedStyle(container).display === 'none') {
            
            return true; // Skip validation if hidden
        }

        var checkboxes = container.querySelectorAll("input[type='checkbox']");
        var isChecked = Array.from(checkboxes).some(checkbox => checkbox.checked);

        if (!isChecked) {
             setError('Please select at least one condition in the Mental Health Questionnaire.');
            return false; 
        }
          var checkBoxOther = document.getElementById('MHQConditionsOther');
          var textAreaOther = document.getElementById('OtherComments');

        if (checkBoxOther && checkBoxOther.checked && textAreaOther && textAreaOther.value.trim() === '') {
             setError('Please provide details in the Other comments section.');
            textAreaOther.focus();
            return false; 
        }
        return true;
    }

     document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', function() {
        var form = document.querySelector('form');
        if (form) {
            form.addEventListener('submit', function(event) {
                var errorContainer = document.getElementById('errorMessageContainer');
                if (errorContainer) {
                    errorContainer.textContent = ''; 
                }
                
                if (!validateMentalHealthSelection()) {
                    event.preventDefault();
                }
            });
        }
    });
    
var Page_ValidationActive = false;
if (typeof(ValidatorOnLoad) == "function") {
    ValidatorOnLoad();
}

function ValidatorOnSubmit() {
    if (Page_ValidationActive) {
        return ValidatorCommonOnSubmit();
    }
    else {
        return true;
    }
}

        <script>
            function HasError() {
                var isValid = Page_ClientValidate();
                if (!isValid) {
                    var spans = document.getElementsByClassName("requiredField");
                    for (let i = 0; i < spans.length; i++) { if (spans[i].style.display.toLowerCase() != 'none') {  window.scrollTo(0,window.scrollY + spans[i].getBoundingClientRect().top ); break; } }
                }
                return !isValid;
                
            }
            
        </script>
